Brief: The handoff runner put "/skillname" as the prompt's first line for every task with a slashCommand in task-map.json. Claude Code CLI parses a leading "/" as a slash-command lookup; because this host has no .claude/commands/ registration, the CLI immediately printed "Unknown command: /X" and exited before reading the prompt body. 4 of 8 already-fired Fri handoff tasks were destroyed this way.

Root cause:C:\Users\tessf\code\codex-lab\.agents\scheduled-claude\handoff-2026-08-07\run-claude-handoff-goal.ps1 line 166: $slashLine = if ($slash) { "$slash`n" } else { '' } — raw slash injected on prompt line 1.

Invoke it via the Skill tool AFTER reading the safety files below. Do not type it as a slash-command...". Line 1 of every future prompt now begins with "Skill", never "/".
